## Integrity and limits

Every unmatched check/path observation appears once above; parent/leaf links and source/reference
policies are not merged into invented defects. Matching controls and all raw callback/source
evidence remain in the pinned archive. Counts describe the unchanged implementation, not a
new repair, a version-aligned comparison or a full-parity acceptance.
